border-radius: 8px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RETURN NODE BUTTON
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root { --cs-return-label: "RETURN NODE"; }

.cs-return {
  position: fixed;
  right: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-right, 12px));
  bottom: max(18px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 18px));
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 52% 48%, rgba(0,255,198,.28) 8%, rgba(0,255,198,.16) 22%, transparent 60%), rgba(10,14,20,.40);
  box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(0,255,198,.28);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  opacity: .85;
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 5000;
  transition: transform .25s ease, box-shadow .25s ease, opacity .25s ease;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.cs-return:hover {
  transform: scale(1.08);
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0,255,198,.45);
}

.cs-return::before {
  content: "⟡";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--mint);
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--mint-glow);
}

.cs-return::after {
  content: var(--cs-return-label);
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: calc(72px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  padding: .25rem .5rem;
  font: 600 .70rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .12em;
  color: #cdfef0;
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.60);
  border: 1px solid var(--mint-border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.cs-return:hover::after {
  opacity: .95;
}

body.admin-bar .cs-return {
  bottom: calc(40px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
}

@media (hover: none) {
  .cs-return {
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
  }
  .cs-return::after {
    font-size: .65rem;
    bottom: 64px;
    opacity: .9;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - TEXT COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.text-mint, .text-mint * { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.text-rose, .text-rose * { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.text-gold, .text-gold * { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.text-indigo, .text-indigo * { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.text-ice, .text-ice * { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.stroke, .stroke * {
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - LINK COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.links-mint a { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.links-rose a { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.links-gold a { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.links-indigo a { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.links-ice a { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.links-mint a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.4); }
.links-rose a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,199,230,.35); }
.links-gold a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,230,163,.35); }
.links-indigo a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(197,201,255,.35); }
.links-ice a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(231,247,255,.35); }

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SECTION THEMES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.theme-mint { --accent: var(--mint); }
.theme-rose { --accent: var(--rose); }
.theme-gold { --accent: var(--gold); }
.theme-indigo { --accent: var(--indigo); }
.theme-ice { --accent: var(--ice); }

.theme-mint h1, .theme-mint h2, .theme-mint h3,
.theme-rose h1, .theme-rose h2, .theme-rose h3,
.theme-gold h1, .theme-gold h2, .theme-gold h3,
.theme-indigo h1, .theme-indigo h2, .theme-indigo h3,
.theme-ice h1, .theme-ice h2, .theme-ice h3 {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
}

.theme-mint .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-rose .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-gold .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-indigo .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-ice .wp-block-button__link {
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.65) !important;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent) 35%, transparent) !important;
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
  border-radius: 10px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SAFETY OVERRIDES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.has-black-color, .has-contrast-color, .has-primary-color, .has-text-color {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* fine-tune return node placement */
.cs-return {
  bottom: 18px !important;  /* move it slightly farther up from bottom edge */
  right: 18px !important;   /* tiny nudge inward from right edge */
}

/* ——— bring the EPISODES above the banner ——— */

.episodes-panel,
.episodes-menu,
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;      /* higher than your nav-top (1500), lower than return node (5000) */
  isolation: isolate !important; /* prevents parent stacking contexts from trapping it */
}

/* ——— put the EPISODES above the banner ——— */

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAV BAR FIELD — unified black block with glowing blue dots
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top-wrapper {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2000;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at center,
    rgba(5,10,20,1) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,1) 100%) !important;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(169,255,232,0.25);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(169,255,232,0.15) inset;
  padding: 0.75rem 0 !important;
  isolation: isolate;
}

/* dotted luminous edge effect */
.nav-top-wrapper::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,0.35) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 16px 16px;
  opacity: 0.25;
  pointer-events: none;
  mask-image: linear-gradient(to bottom, black 80%, transparent 100%);
  z-index: 0;
}

/* ensure nav content stays crisp above */
.nav-top-wrapper > * {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 5;
}

.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::before,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::after,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation:first-of-type::before,

}

/* keep the episodes column safely above everything */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]),

/* ◇ Mint bead rows for the black veil strip (top + bottom) */
.cs-veil-bg::before,
.cs-veil-bg::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: -12px;                   /* extend slightly past the edges for a soft overhang */
  right: -12px;
  height: 12px;                  /* thickness of the dotted row */
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.75) 2px, transparent 3px);
  background-size: 18px 12px;    /* spacing of dots (x = gap, y = row height) */
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(169,255,232,.45));  /* gentle glow */
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 1;                    /* above the animated bg, below the text line */
}

.cs-veil-bg::before { top: -10px; }    /* top row just outside the bar */
.cs-veil-bg::after  { bottom: -10px; } /* bottom row just outside the bar */

/* keep the status text above the dots */
.cs-veil-line { position: relative; z-index: 2; }

/* optional: slightly round the ends of the strip so the beads “hug” it */
.cs-veil { border-radius: 8px; }

we’ll (1) silence the old bead rows, then (2) add a true inner **halo** that always shows—no overflow fights, nice mint glow, and (3) give you an optional slow drift.

### paste this EXACTLY at the very end of your CSS
(it safely disables the old attempt and installs the halo)

```css
/* ——— turn off the earlier top/bottom bead rows ——— */
.cs-veil-bg::before,
.cs-veil-bg::after {
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* ——— full inner dotted halo around the veil bar ——— */
.cs-veil {
  position: relative !important;
  overflow: hidden;            /* keeps halo neatly inside the bar */
  border-radius: 10px;         /* feel free to tweak */
}

/* the mint constellation ring */
.cs-veil::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;                    /* match the veil’s rectangle */

---

padding: 8px;                /* ← halo thickness (change to taste) */
  border-radius: 12px;         /* gentle rounding to hug the bar */

/* dot field + gentle glow */
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.85) 2px, transparent 3px) 0 0/18px 18px;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(169,255,232,.45));

/* mask trick: keep dots only in the ring area (border band) */
  -webkit-mask:
    linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box,
    linear-gradient(#000 0 0);
  -webkit-mask-composite: xor;
          mask-composite: exclude;

pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 2;                  /* above animated bg, below the text */
}

/* keep the status line safely above the halo */
.cs-veil-line { position: relative; z-index: 3 !important; }
.cs-veil-bg   { position: absolute; inset: -1px; z-index: 1 !important; }

/* ——— optional: slow, barely-there drift of the star beads ——— */
@keyframes csHaloDrift {
  to { background-position: 18px 18px; }
}
.cs-veil::before {
  animation: csHaloDrift 60s linear infinite;
  /* remove the animation line above if you want the halo static */
}
```

### if it still doesn’t show
- make sure this block is **below** any earlier veil styles in “Additional CSS”.
- hard-refresh (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+R) to bust cache.

## Option A (cleanest): add a class in the editor

Then paste this **at the very end** of your CSS:

```css
/* ——— remove the veil halo we added ——— */
.cs-veil::before { content: none !important; }

/* ——— dotted mint halo around the TOP NAV wrapper ——— */
.nav-top-halo {
  position: relative !important;

  isolation: isolate;            /* keep its own stacking context */
}
.nav-top-halo::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  padding: 8px;                  /* ← halo thickness */
  border-radius: 12px;

/* dot field + glow */

    radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.85) 2px, transparent 3px) 0 0/18px 18px;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(169,255,232,.45));

    linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box,
    linear-gradient(#000 0 0);
  -webkit-mask-composite: xor;
          mask-composite: exclude;

pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 1;                    /* under nav links, above any bg */
}

/* keep nav links above the halo ring */
.nav-top-halo > * { position: relative; z-index: 2; }

/* optional slow drift */

.nav-top-halo::before { animation: navHaloDrift 60s linear infinite; }
```

```css
}
```

```css
/* simple dotted halo around the black strip */
.cs-veil { position: relative !important; }

.cs-veil-halo {
  position: absolute !important;
  inset: 4px !important;              /* how far in from the edge */
  border: 2px dotted #a9ffe8 !important; /* mint dots */
  border-radius: 10px !important;     /* match your strip corners */
  opacity: .7 !important;
```

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CRYPTIC SYNDICATE • CLEAN THEME CSS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* ───── COLOR TOKENS ───── */
:root {
  --mint: #a9ffe8;
  --mint-strong: #6fffe1;
  --mint-glow: rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  --rose: #ffc7e6;
  --gold: #ffe6a3;
  --indigo: #c5c9ff;
  --ice: #e7f7ff;

--cs-purple: #8b7ab8;
  --cs-purple-hover: #a99dd4;

--glass: rgba(10,14,20,.65);
  --glass-hover: rgba(10,14,20,.78);
  --mint-border: rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  --stroke: rgba(0,0,0,.70);
  --ink: rgba(0,0,0,.7);
  --glow: rgba(255,255,255,.18);
}

/* ───── GLOBAL BASE ───── */
html, body, .wp-site-blocks {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

/* ───── DEFAULT TEXT STYLING ───── */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
  color: var(--indigo) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px rgba(197, 201, 255, 0.35), 0 0 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.20);
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--mint-glow), 0 0 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.20);
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* ───── LINKS ───── */
a, a:visited {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.45);
}
a:hover, a:focus {
  color: var(--mint-strong) !important;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.75);
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--mint-glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAVIGATION - PURPLE TEXT, NO BACKGROUNDS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Target your top nav with class "nav-top" on the Navigation block */
.nav-top,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;
}

/* All top nav links = purple, no backgrounds */
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item > a,
.nav-top a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  text-shadow: none !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0 !important;
  padding: .3rem .6rem !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}

.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover a,
.nav-top a:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

/* Nav icons */
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation svg {
  fill: var(--cs-purple) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EPISODES DROPDOWN - BLACK BACKGROUND
   Add class "episodes-menu" to your Episodes submenu
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* The dropdown container itself */
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container,
.episodes-menu > ul {
  background: #000 !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  padding: 16px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6) !important;
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
}

/* Episode links inside */
.episodes-menu a,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-link__content {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;

---

background: transparent !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  padding: .5rem .75rem !important;
  border-radius: 6px !important;
  display: block !important;
}

.episodes-menu a:hover,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-link__content:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   REGULAR DROPDOWNS (non-episodes) - TRANSPARENT
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) {
  background: rgba(10, 14, 20, 0.85) !important;
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px) !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.25) !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
  padding: .35rem 0 !important;
}

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  padding: .45rem .75rem !important;
}

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.12) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   FOOTER - PURPLE TEXT, NO BACKGROUNDS
   Add class "footer-links" to your footer Navigation block
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.footer-links,
footer .footer-links {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;
}

.footer-links a,
.footer-links .wp-block-button__link,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  text-shadow: none !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0 !important;
  padding: .45rem .9rem !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}

.footer-links a:hover,
.footer-links .wp-block-button__link:hover,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover a {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.12) !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RETURN NODE BUTTON
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root { --cs-return-label: "RETURN NODE"; }

.cs-return {
  position: fixed;
  right: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-right, 12px));
  bottom: max(18px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 18px));
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 52% 48%, rgba(0,255,198,.28) 8%, rgba(0,255,198,.16) 22%, transparent 60%), rgba(10,14,20,.40);
  box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(0,255,198,.28);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  opacity: .85;
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 5000;
  transition: transform .25s ease, box-shadow .25s ease, opacity .25s ease;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.cs-return:hover {
  transform: scale(1.08);
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0,255,198,.45);
}

.cs-return::before {
  content: "⟡";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--mint);
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--mint-glow);
}

.cs-return::after {
  content: var(--cs-return-label);
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: calc(72px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  padding: .25rem .5rem;
  font: 600 .70rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .12em;
  color: #cdfef0;
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.60);
  border: 1px solid var(--mint-border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.cs-return:hover::after {
  opacity: .95;
}

body.admin-bar .cs-return {
  bottom: calc(40px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
}

@media (hover: none) {
  .cs-return {
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
  }
  .cs-return::after {
    font-size: .65rem;
    bottom: 64px;
    opacity: .9;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - TEXT COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.text-mint, .text-mint * { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.text-rose, .text-rose * { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.text-gold, .text-gold * { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.text-indigo, .text-indigo * { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.text-ice, .text-ice * { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.stroke, .stroke * {
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - LINK COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.links-mint a { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.links-rose a { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.links-gold a { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.links-indigo a { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.links-ice a { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.links-mint a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.4); }
.links-rose a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,199,230,.35); }
.links-gold a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,230,163,.35); }
.links-indigo a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(197,201,255,.35); }
.links-ice a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(231,247,255,.35); }

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SECTION THEMES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.theme-mint { --accent: var(--mint); }
.theme-rose { --accent: var(--rose); }
.theme-gold { --accent: var(--gold); }
.theme-indigo { --accent: var(--indigo); }
.theme-ice { --accent: var(--ice); }

.theme-mint h1, .theme-mint h2, .theme-mint h3,
.theme-rose h1, .theme-rose h2, .theme-rose h3,
.theme-gold h1, .theme-gold h2, .theme-gold h3,
.theme-indigo h1, .theme-indigo h2, .theme-indigo h3,
.theme-ice h1, .theme-ice h2, .theme-ice h3 {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
}

.theme-mint .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-rose .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-gold .wp-block-button__link,

---

.theme-indigo .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-ice .wp-block-button__link {
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.65) !important;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent) 35%, transparent) !important;
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
  border-radius: 10px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SAFETY OVERRIDES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.has-black-color, .has-contrast-color, .has-primary-color, .has-text-color {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* fine-tune return node placement */
.cs-return {
  bottom: 18px !important;  /* move it slightly farther up from bottom edge */
  right: 18px !important;   /* tiny nudge inward from right edge */
}

/* ——— bring the EPISODES above the banner ——— */
/* use whichever you have: episodes-panel (sidebar) or episodes-menu (dropdown) */

.episodes-panel,
.episodes-menu,
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;      /* higher than your nav-top (1500), lower than return node (5000) */
  isolation: isolate !important; /* prevents parent stacking contexts from trapping it */
}

/* ——— put the EPISODES above the banner ——— */
/* works whether you used episodes-panel (sidebar) or episodes-menu (dropdown) */

.episodes-panel,
.episodes-menu,
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;        /* higher than nav/header (you set ~1500), below return node (5000) */
  isolation: isolate !important;   /* escape parent stacking contexts (filters/transforms/opacity) */
}

/* if you used the ::before black slab, keep it behind the links but within the raised stack */
.episodes-panel::before {
  z-index: -1 !important;
}

/* gently lower the banner/header so it doesn’t eclipse the episodes */
header,
.site-header,
.wp-block-template-part__header,
.wp-block-cover.is-position-sticky {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 1200 !important;        /* still above content, but below episodes (3000) */
}

/* if your episodes are auto-detected (no class), raise those too */
body .wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/episode-"]),
body .wp-block-group:has(.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/episode-"]) {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;
  isolation: isolate !important;
}

/* optional: tiny breathing room under the banner */
.episodes-panel { margin-top: 10px !important; }

/* OPTION A: solid black */
.episodes-panel::before{
  content:"" !important;
  position:absolute !important;
  inset:-10px -12px !important; /* margin around links */
  background:#000 !important;    /* << change color here */
  border:1px solid rgba(139,122,184,.35) !important;
  border-radius:12px !important;
  box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.45) !important;
  z-index:-1 !important;         /* sits behind the links */
  mix-blend-mode:normal !important;
  opacity:1 !important;
}

/* Episodes dropdown menu panel */
.episodes-menu,
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container{
  background:#000 !important; /* or the same gradient as above */
  border:1px solid rgba(139,122,184,.35) !important;
  border-radius:12px !important;
  box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.6) !important;
}

/* DEBUG — highlight the episodes container */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) {
  outline: 2px dashed hotpink !important;
}

/* EPISODES — force dark background for side list AND dropdown */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]) {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;           /* above banner */
  isolation: isolate !important;
}

/* slab behind the side list */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"])::before {
  content: "" !important;
  position: absolute !important;
  inset: -10px -12px !important;      /* breathing room */
  background: #000 !important;        /* ← change to deep blue if you want */
  /* background: linear-gradient(180deg,#0b1020 0%,#02040a 100%) !important; */
  border: 1px solid rgba(139,122,184,.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.45) !important;
  z-index: -1 !important;             /* behind the links */
  mix-blend-mode: normal !important;
}

/* keep the episode links readable inside */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]) a {
  display: block;
  padding: .5rem .75rem !important;
  border-radius: 6px;
  color: #a9ffe8 !important;          /* mint on dark */
  background: transparent !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover {
  color: #a99dd4 !important;          /* lavender hover */
  background: rgba(255,255,255,.06) !important;
}

/* if your episodes show in a NAV dropdown (submenu), tint that panel too */
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) {
  background: #000 !important;        /* or the same gradient */
  border: 1px solid rgba(139,122,184,.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.6) !important;
}
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a {
  color: #a9ffe8 !important;
}

/* === 1) FIND THE OUTER NAV THAT CONTAINS EITHER EPISODES OR MODERN GODS === */

---

nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/the-council-of-the-unnamed"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/six-in-view"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/death"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/zodiac-receded"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/time"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/light"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/fear"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/silence"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/love"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/dissonance"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/finally-found"]),
/* sometimes the nav is wrapped in a Group */
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/modern-gods"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/the-council-of-the-unnamed"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/six-in-view"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/death"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/zodiac-receded"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/time"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/light"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/fear"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/silence"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/love"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/dissonance"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/finally-found"]) {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;         /* above banner */
  isolation: isolate !important;
}

/* put ONE shared black slab behind the WHOLE column */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/the-council-of-the-unnamed"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/six-in-view"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/death"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/zodiac-receded"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/time"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/light"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/fear"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/silence"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/love"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/dissonance"])::before,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/finally-found"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/modern-gods"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/the-council-of-the-unnamed"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/six-in-view"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/death"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/zodiac-receded"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/time"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/light"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/fear"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/silence"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/love"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/dissonance"])::before,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/finally-found"])::before {
  content: "" !important;
  position: absolute !important;
  inset: -10px -12px !important;     /* margin around both submenus */
  background: #000 !important;       /* change to gradient if you prefer */
  border: 1px solid rgba(139,122,184,.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.45) !important;
  z-index: -1 !important;            /* stays behind links */
}

/* keep links readable inside the panel (both groups) */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]) a,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]) a,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/modern-gods"]) a {
  color: #a9ffe8 !important;         /* mint */
  text-decoration: none !important;
  display: block;
  padding: .5rem .75rem !important;
  border-radius: 6px;
}
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover,
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]) a:hover,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]) a:hover,
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/modern-gods"]) a:hover {
  color: #a99dd4 !important;         /* lavender hover */
  background: rgba(255,255,255,.06) !important;
}

/* when either submenu opens as a dropdown, make its panel dark too */
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/the-council-of-the-unnamed"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/six-in-view"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/death"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/zodiac-receded"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/time"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/light"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/fear"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/silence"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/love"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/dissonance"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/finally-found"]) {
  background: #000 !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139,122,184,.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.6) !important;
}
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]) a,
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]) a {
  color: #a9ffe8 !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAV BAR FIELD — unified black block with glowing blue dots
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top-wrapper {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2000;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at center,
    rgba(5,10,20,1) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,1) 100%) !important;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(169,255,232,0.25);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(169,255,232,0.15) inset;
  padding: 0.75rem 0 !important;
  isolation: isolate;
}

/* dotted luminous edge effect */
.nav-top-wrapper::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,0.35) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 16px 16px;
  opacity: 0.25;
  pointer-events: none;
  mask-image: linear-gradient(to bottom, black 80%, transparent 100%);
  z-index: 0;
}

/* ensure nav content stays crisp above */
.nav-top-wrapper > * {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 5;
}

/* ───── HARD ROLLBACK: remove all header slabs & debug outlines ───── */

/* kill any global fixed strips */
body::before,
body::after{
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* kill any nav pseudo-layers */
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::before,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::after,

.cs-veil-bg::after  { bottom: -10px; } /* bottom row just outside the bar */

## 1) Add one line to your header HTML

## 2) Replace the bead CSS with a simple, unmissable halo
In **Additional CSS**, do NOT delete anything else. Just paste the block below at the very **end** so it overrides earlier rules.

```css
/* ——— TURN OFF the old bead rows that didn’t show ——— */
.cs-veil-bg::before,
.cs-veil-bg::after {
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* ——— SIMPLE HALO around the black strip ——— */
.cs-veil { position: relative !important; }

.cs-veil-halo {
  position: absolute !important;
  inset: 4px !important;                 /* distance from the strip edge */
  border: 2px dotted #a9ffe8 !important; /* mint dotted ring */
  border-radius: 10px !important;        /* match your strip corners */
  opacity: .8 !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 8px rgba(169,255,232,.35),
    inset 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.15) !important;
  pointer-events: none !important;
  z-index: 2 !important;                 /* above bg, below the text */
}

/* keep layers sane */
.cs-veil-bg   { position: absolute; inset: -1px; z-index: 0 !important; }
.cs-veil-line { position: relative; z-index: 3 !important; }

/* OPTIONAL: quick sanity check — uncomment to see the strip box
.cs-veil { outline: 2px dashed #a9ffe8 !important; }
*/
```

- Save the Header template, save Additional CSS, then hard-refresh (Ctrl/Cmd-Shift-R).
- Want the ring tighter/looser? tweak `inset: 4px`.
- Want chunkier dots? bump `border: 3px dotted #6fffe1`.

  (function(){
    const lines = [

      "[Echo nodes: online]",

    let i = 0;

function glitch() {
      el.style.transform = `translate(${Math.random()*4-2}px, ${Math.random()*2-1}px)`;
      el.style.filter = 'hue-rotate(180deg)';
      setTimeout(() => {
        el.style.transform = '';
        el.style.filter = '';
      }, 50);
    }

function swap(){
      // Random glitch 20% of the time
      if(Math.random() < 0.2) glitch();

i = (i+1) % lines.length;
      el.style.opacity = 0;
      setTimeout(()=>{ el.textContent = lines[i]; el.style.opacity = 1; }, 220);
    }
    setInterval(swap, 4200);
  })();
</script>

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CRYPTIC SYNDICATE • CLEAN THEME CSS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* ───── COLOR TOKENS ───── */
:root {
  --mint: #a9ffe8;
  --mint-strong: #6fffe1;
  --mint-glow: rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  --rose: #ffc7e6;
  --gold: #ffe6a3;
  --indigo: #c5c9ff;
  --ice: #e7f7ff;

--cs-purple: #8b7ab8;
  --cs-purple-hover: #a99dd4;

--glass: rgba(10,14,20,.65);
  --glass-hover: rgba(10,14,20,.78);
  --mint-border: rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  --stroke: rgba(0,0,0,.70);
  --ink: rgba(0,0,0,.7);
  --glow: rgba(255,255,255,.18);
}

/* ───── GLOBAL BASE ───── */
html, body, .wp-site-blocks {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

/* ───── DEFAULT TEXT STYLING ───── */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
  color: var(--indigo) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px rgba(197, 201, 255, 0.35), 0 0 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.20);
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--mint-glow), 0 0 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.20);
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* ───── LINKS ───── */
a, a:visited {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.45);
}
a:hover, a:focus {
  color: var(--mint-strong) !important;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.75);
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--mint-glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAVIGATION - PURPLE TEXT, NO BACKGROUNDS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Target your top nav with class "nav-top" on the Navigation block */
.nav-top,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;
}

/* All top nav links = purple, no backgrounds */
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item > a,
.nav-top a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  text-shadow: none !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0 !important;
  padding: .3rem .6rem !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}

.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover a,
.nav-top a:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

/* Nav icons */
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation svg {
  fill: var(--cs-purple) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EPISODES DROPDOWN - BLACK BACKGROUND
   Add class "episodes-menu" to your Episodes submenu
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* The dropdown container itself */
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container,
.episodes-menu > ul {
  background: #000 !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  padding: 16px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6) !important;
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
}

/* Episode links inside */
.episodes-menu a,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-link__content {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  padding: .5rem .75rem !important;
  border-radius: 6px !important;
  display: block !important;
}

.episodes-menu a:hover,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-link__content:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   REGULAR DROPDOWNS (non-episodes) - TRANSPARENT
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) {

---

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.footer-links,
footer .footer-links {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;

.footer-links a,
.footer-links .wp-block-button__link,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content,

  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;

  box-shadow: none !important;

}

.footer-links a:hover,

.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover a {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.12) !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RETURN NODE BUTTON
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root { --cs-return-label: "RETURN NODE"; }

.cs-return {
  position: fixed;
  right: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-right, 12px));
  bottom: max(18px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 18px));
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 52% 48%, rgba(0,255,198,.28) 8%, rgba(0,255,198,.16) 22%, transparent 60%), rgba(10,14,20,.40);
  box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(0,255,198,.28);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  opacity: .85;
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 5000;
  transition: transform .25s ease, box-shadow .25s ease, opacity .25s ease;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.cs-return:hover {
  transform: scale(1.08);
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0,255,198,.45);
}

.cs-return::before {
  content: "⟡";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--mint);
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--mint-glow);
}

.cs-return::after {
  content: var(--cs-return-label);
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: calc(72px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  padding: .25rem .5rem;
  font: 600 .70rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .12em;
  color: #cdfef0;
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.60);
  border: 1px solid var(--mint-border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.cs-return:hover::after {
  opacity: .95;
}

body.admin-bar .cs-return {
  bottom: calc(40px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
}

@media (hover: none) {
  .cs-return {
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
  }
  .cs-return::after {
    font-size: .65rem;
    bottom: 64px;
    opacity: .9;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - TEXT COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.text-mint, .text-mint * { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.text-rose, .text-rose * { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.text-gold, .text-gold * { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.text-indigo, .text-indigo * { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.text-ice, .text-ice * { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.stroke, .stroke * {
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - LINK COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.links-mint a { color: var(--mint) !important; }

.links-gold a { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.links-indigo a { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.links-ice a { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.links-mint a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.4); }
.links-rose a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,199,230,.35); }
.links-gold a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,230,163,.35); }
.links-indigo a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(197,201,255,.35); }
.links-ice a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(231,247,255,.35); }

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SECTION THEMES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.theme-mint { --accent: var(--mint); }
.theme-rose { --accent: var(--rose); }
.theme-gold { --accent: var(--gold); }
.theme-indigo { --accent: var(--indigo); }
.theme-ice { --accent: var(--ice); }

.theme-mint h1, .theme-mint h2, .theme-mint h3,
.theme-rose h1, .theme-rose h2, .theme-rose h3,
.theme-gold h1, .theme-gold h2, .theme-gold h3,
.theme-indigo h1, .theme-indigo h2, .theme-indigo h3,
.theme-ice h1, .theme-ice h2, .theme-ice h3 {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
}

.theme-mint .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-rose .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-gold .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-indigo .wp-block-button__link,
.theme-ice .wp-block-button__link {
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.65) !important;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent) 35%, transparent) !important;
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
  border-radius: 10px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SAFETY OVERRIDES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.has-black-color, .has-contrast-color, .has-primary-color, .has-text-color {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* fine-tune return node placement */

---

.cs-return {
  bottom: 18px !important;  /* move it slightly farther up from bottom edge */
  right: 18px !important;   /* tiny nudge inward from right edge */
}

/* ——— bring the EPISODES above the banner ——— */

.episodes-panel,
.episodes-menu,
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;      /* higher than your nav-top (1500), lower than return node (5000) */
  isolation: isolate !important; /* prevents parent stacking contexts from trapping it */
}

/* ——— put the EPISODES above the banner ——— */
/* works whether you used episodes-panel (sidebar) or episodes-menu (dropdown) */

.episodes-panel,
.episodes-menu,
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {

  z-index: 3000 !important;        /* higher than nav/header (you set ~1500), below return node (5000) */

/* if you used the ::before black slab, keep it behind the links but within the raised stack */

/* gently lower the banner/header so it doesn’t eclipse the episodes */
header,

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAV BAR FIELD — unified black block with glowing blue dots
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top-wrapper {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2000;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at center,
    rgba(5,10,20,1) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,1) 100%) !important;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(169,255,232,0.25);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(169,255,232,0.15) inset;
  padding: 0.75rem 0 !important;
  isolation: isolate;
}

/* dotted luminous edge effect */
.nav-top-wrapper::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,0.35) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 16px 16px;
  opacity: 0.25;
  pointer-events: none;
  mask-image: linear-gradient(to bottom, black 80%, transparent 100%);
  z-index: 0;
}

/* ensure nav content stays crisp above */
.nav-top-wrapper > * {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 5;
}

/* ───── HARD ROLLBACK: remove all header slabs & debug outlines ───── */

/* kill any global fixed strips */
body::before,
body::after{
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* kill any nav pseudo-layers */
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::before,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::after,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation:first-of-type::before,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation:first-of-type::after{
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* kill the pink debug outlines for the episode/modern-gods selectors */

ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]){

}

/* keep the episodes column safely above everything */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/modern-gods"]){
  position: relative !important;

/* ◇ Mint bead rows for the black veil strip (top + bottom) */
.cs-veil-bg::before,
.cs-veil-bg::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: -12px;                   /* extend slightly past the edges for a soft overhang */
  right: -12px;
  height: 12px;                  /* thickness of the dotted row */
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.75) 2px, transparent 3px);
  background-size: 18px 12px;    /* spacing of dots (x = gap, y = row height) */
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(169,255,232,.45));  /* gentle glow */
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 1;                    /* above the animated bg, below the text line */
}

.cs-veil-bg::before { top: -10px; }    /* top row just outside the bar */
.cs-veil-bg::after  { bottom: -10px; } /* bottom row just outside the bar */

/* keep the status text above the dots */
.cs-veil-line { position: relative; z-index: 2; }

/* optional: slightly round the ends of the strip so the beads “hug” it */
.cs-veil { border-radius: 8px; }

/* ——— TURN OFF the old bead rows that didn’t show ——— */

---

.cs-veil-bg::before,
.cs-veil-bg::after {
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* ——— SIMPLE HALO around the black strip ——— */
.cs-veil { position: relative !important; }

.cs-veil-halo {
  position: absolute !important;
  inset: 4px !important;                 /* distance from the strip edge */
  border: 2px dotted #a9ffe8 !important; /* mint dotted ring */
  border-radius: 10px !important;        /* match your strip corners */
  opacity: .8 !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 8px rgba(169,255,232,.35),
    inset 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.15) !important;
  pointer-events: none !important;
  z-index: 2 !important;                 /* above bg, below the text */
}

/* keep layers sane */
.cs-veil-bg   { position: absolute; inset: -1px; z-index: 0 !important; }
.cs-veil-line { position: relative; z-index: 3 !important; }

/* OPTIONAL: quick sanity check — uncomment to see the strip box
.cs-veil { outline: 2px dashed #a9ffe8 !important; }
*/

### paste this **in your header’s `<style>`**, replacing your current `.cs-veil:after` block

---

```css
/* scanlines + tasteful mint bead row only at the bottom edge */
.cs-veil { 
  position: relative;
  border-radius: 10px;
  overflow: hidden;                         /* trims dots at the rounded corners */
}

.cs-veil:after{
  content:"";
  position:absolute;
  inset:0;
  pointer-events:none;
  z-index:1;

/* layers: 1) bottom dots  2) existing scanlines */
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.70) 1.2px, transparent 1.3px)
      bottom left / 14px 11px repeat-x,
    repeating-linear-gradient(0deg,
      rgba(255,255,255,.03) 0 1px,
      transparent 2px 4px);

/* feather the ends so the row fades near the rounded corners */
  -webkit-mask: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 12px, #000 28px calc(100% - 28px), transparent calc(100% - 12px));
          mask: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 12px, #000 28px calc(100% - 28px), transparent calc(100% - 12px));

/* gentle glow under the bead row */
  box-shadow: 0 -1px 0 rgba(169,255,232,.25) inset,
              0 -6px 14px rgba(169,255,232,.12) inset;
}
```

### quick knobs (change → effect)
- `14px 11px` → spacing (x) and row height (y). Smaller x = more beads.
- `1.2px` → bead size. `1.4px` for chunkier; `1px` for finer.
- fade at the ends → tweak the two numbers in `mask`: `12px` (fade width) and `28px` (solid start).
- more/less glow → adjust the two `inset` shadows.

---

#### want **top + bottom**?
Add one extra line to the background list (above the scanlines), so there are *two* bead rows:
```css
radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.70) 1.2px, transparent 1.3px)
  top left / 14px 11px repeat-x,
```

this approach keeps the banner’s color untouched, the dots sit exactly on the edge, and the corners breathe instead of getting crowded. save, hard refresh (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+R), and we should have a clean, mythic shimmer instead of noise.


And same for your “Ancient Future Coalescent” submenu block.

Then, add in your CSS:

```css
  position: relative;
  border: 1px dotted rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 6px 0;

    inset 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.15);
}
```

That gives you a clean mint-glow halo around the submenu area itself.

---

### **Option 2: Wrap the entire navigation column**
If you want the *entire left-side nav list* (both submenus) wrapped as one unit (so the halo encircles the whole list):

```html
<!-- /wp:group -->
```

```css
.cs-nav-wrap {
  position: relative;
  border: 2px dotted #a9ffe8;
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 10px;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 12px rgba(169,255,232,.25),
    inset 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.15);
  background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
}
```

---

💡 **Tip:**  
If WordPress’s visual editor won’t let you insert `<div>` directly, switch to **Code Editor mode** (top right → “⋮” → “Code Editor”) and paste it there.

  /* ——— rotating veil ——— */
  .cs-veil {
    position: relative;
    inset: 0;
    width: 100vw;
    left: 50%;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    min-height: 60px;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    overflow: hidden;
    z-index: 1000;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    background: #0b0b0f;
  }
  .cs-veil-bg {
    position: absolute;
    inset: -1px;
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, #0b0b0f, #151528, #0b0b0f);
    background-size: 200% 100%;
    animation: csShift 18s linear infinite;
    opacity: 0.9;
  }

/* ——— scrolling symbols ——— */
  .cs-veil-symbols {
    position: absolute;
    top: 50%;
    left: 0;
    transform: translateY(-50%);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    color: rgba(182, 255, 228, 0.08);
    white-space: nowrap;
    letter-spacing: 1.5rem;

  @keyframes csSymbolScroll {
    from { transform: translateY(-50%) translateX(0); }

/* gentle glow under the bead row */
```css
  box-shadow:
    0 -1px 0 rgba(169,255,232,.25) inset,
    0 -6px 14px rgba(169,255,232,.12) inset;
}
```

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CRYPTIC SYNDICATE • CLEAN THEME CSS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* ───── COLOR TOKENS ───── */
:root {
  --mint: #a9ffe8;
  --mint-strong: #6fffe1;
  --mint-glow: rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  --rose: #ffc7e6;
  --gold: #ffe6a3;
  --indigo: #c5c9ff;
  --ice: #e7f7ff;

--cs-purple: #8b7ab8;
  --cs-purple-hover: #a99dd4;

--glass: rgba(10,14,20,.65);
  --glass-hover: rgba(10,14,20,.78);
  --mint-border: rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  --stroke: rgba(0,0,0,.70);
  --ink: rgba(0,0,0,.7);
  --glow: rgba(255,255,255,.18);
}

/* ───── GLOBAL BASE ───── */
html, body, .wp-site-blocks {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

/* ───── DEFAULT TEXT STYLING ───── */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
  color: var(--indigo) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px rgba(197, 201, 255, 0.35), 0 0 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.20);
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

p, li, dt, dd, blockquote, cite, figcaption {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--mint-glow), 0 0 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.20);
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--stroke);
}

/* ───── LINKS ───── */
a, a:visited {
  color: var(--mint) !important;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.45);
}
a:hover, a:focus {
  color: var(--mint-strong) !important;
  text-decoration-color: rgba(169,255,232,.75);
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--mint-glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAVIGATION - PURPLE TEXT, NO BACKGROUNDS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Target your top nav with class "nav-top" on the Navigation block */
.nav-top,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;
}

/* All top nav links = purple, no backgrounds */
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content a,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation__container > .wp-block-navigation-item > a,
.nav-top a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  text-shadow: none !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0 !important;
  padding: .3rem .6rem !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}

.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
.nav-top .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover a,
.nav-top a:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

/* Nav icons */

---

.nav-top .wp-block-navigation svg {
  fill: var(--cs-purple) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EPISODES DROPDOWN - BLACK BACKGROUND
   Add class "episodes-menu" to your Episodes submenu
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* The dropdown container itself */
.episodes-menu.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container,
.episodes-menu > ul {
  background: #000 !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.35) !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  padding: 16px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6) !important;
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
}

/* Episode links inside */
.episodes-menu a,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-link__content {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  padding: .5rem .75rem !important;
  border-radius: 6px !important;
  display: block !important;
}

.episodes-menu a:hover,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover,
.episodes-menu .wp-block-navigation-link__content:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   REGULAR DROPDOWNS (non-episodes) - TRANSPARENT
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) {
  background: rgba(10, 14, 20, 0.85) !important;
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px) !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.25) !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
  padding: .35rem 0 !important;
}

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  padding: .45rem .75rem !important;
}

.nav-top ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:not(.episodes-menu) a:hover {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.12) !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   FOOTER - PURPLE TEXT, NO BACKGROUNDS
   Add class "footer-links" to your footer Navigation block
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.footer-links,
footer .footer-links {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1500;
}

.footer-links a,
.footer-links .wp-block-button__link,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content a {
  color: var(--cs-purple) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
  border: none !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  text-shadow: none !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: 0 !important;
  padding: .45rem .9rem !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
}

.footer-links a:hover,
.footer-links .wp-block-button__link:hover,
.footer-links .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover a {
  color: var(--cs-purple-hover) !important;
  background: rgba(139, 122, 184, 0.12) !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RETURN NODE BUTTON
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root { --cs-return-label: "RETURN NODE"; }

.cs-return {
  position: fixed;
  right: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-right, 12px));
  bottom: max(18px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 18px));
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 52% 48%, rgba(0,255,198,.28) 8%, rgba(0,255,198,.16) 22%, transparent 60%), rgba(10,14,20,.40);
  box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(0,255,198,.28);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  opacity: .85;
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 5000;
  transition: transform .25s ease, box-shadow .25s ease, opacity .25s ease;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.cs-return:hover {
  transform: scale(1.08);
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(0,255,198,.45);
}

.cs-return::before {
  content: "⟡";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--mint);
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  text-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--mint-glow);
}

.cs-return::after {
  content: var(--cs-return-label);
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: calc(72px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  padding: .25rem .5rem;
  font: 600 .70rem ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  letter-spacing: .12em;
  color: #cdfef0;
  background: rgba(10,14,20,.60);
  border: 1px solid var(--mint-border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity .25s ease;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.cs-return:hover::after {
  opacity: .95;
}

body.admin-bar .cs-return {
  bottom: calc(40px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px));
}

@media (hover: none) {
  .cs-return {
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
  }
  .cs-return::after {
    font-size: .65rem;
    bottom: 64px;
    opacity: .9;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - TEXT COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.text-mint, .text-mint * { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.text-rose, .text-rose * { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.text-gold, .text-gold * { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.text-indigo, .text-indigo * { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.text-ice, .text-ice * { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.stroke, .stroke * {
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
  text-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--glow);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY CLASSES - LINK COLORS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.links-mint a { color: var(--mint) !important; }
.links-rose a { color: var(--rose) !important; }
.links-gold a { color: var(--gold) !important; }
.links-indigo a { color: var(--indigo) !important; }
.links-ice a { color: var(--ice) !important; }

.links-mint a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.4); }
.links-rose a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,199,230,.35); }
.links-gold a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,230,163,.35); }
.links-indigo a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(197,201,255,.35); }
.links-ice a:hover { text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(231,247,255,.35); }

---

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SECTION THEMES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.theme-mint { --accent: var(--mint); }
.theme-rose { --accent: var(--rose); }
.theme-gold { --accent: var(--gold); }
.theme-indigo { --accent: var(--indigo); }
.theme-ice { --accent: var(--ice); }

.theme-mint h1, .theme-mint h2, .theme-mint h3,
.theme-rose h1, .theme-rose h2, .theme-rose h3,
.theme-gold h1, .theme-gold h2, .theme-gold h3,
.theme-indigo h1, .theme-indigo h2, .theme-indigo h3,
.theme-ice h1, .theme-ice h2, .theme-ice h3 {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  -webkit-text-stroke: .35px var(--ink);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TOP NAV BAR FIELD — unified black block with glowing blue dots
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

.nav-top-wrapper {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2000;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at center,
    rgba(5,10,20,1) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,1) 100%) !important;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(169,255,232,0.25);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(169,255,232,0.15) inset;
  padding: 0.75rem 0 !important;
  isolation: isolate;
}

/* dotted luminous edge effect */
.nav-top-wrapper::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,0.35) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 16px 16px;
  opacity: 0.25;
  pointer-events: none;
  mask-image: linear-gradient(to bottom, black 80%, transparent 100%);
  z-index: 0;
}

/* ensure nav content stays crisp above */
.nav-top-wrapper > * {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 5;
}

/* ───── HARD ROLLBACK: remove all header slabs & debug outlines ───── */

/* kill any global fixed strips */
body::before,
body::after{
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* kill any nav pseudo-layers */
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::before,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation::after,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation:first-of-type::before,
.wp-site-blocks header .wp-block-navigation:first-of-type::after{
  content: none !important;
  display: none !important;
}

/* kill the pink debug outlines for the episode/modern-gods selectors */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/modern-gods"]),
ul.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]){
  outline: none !important;
}

/* keep the episodes column safely above everything */
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href^="/episode-"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href^="/episode-"]),
nav.wp-block-navigation:has(a[href*="/modern-gods"]),
.wp-block-group:has(nav.wp-block-navigation a[href*="/modern-gods"]){
  position: relative !important;
  z-index: 3000 !important;
  isolation: isolate !important;
}

/* ◇ Mint bead rows for the black veil strip (top + bottom) */
.cs-veil-bg::before,
.cs-veil-bg::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: -12px;                   /* extend slightly past the edges for a soft overhang */

---

right: -12px;
  height: 12px;                  /* thickness of the dotted row */
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.75) 2px, transparent 3px);
  background-size: 18px 12px;    /* spacing of dots (x = gap, y = row height) */
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(169,255,232,.45));  /* gentle glow */
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 1;                    /* above the animated bg, below the text line */
}

.cs-veil-bg::before { top: -10px; }    /* top row just outside the bar */
.cs-veil-bg::after  { bottom: -10px; } /* bottom row just outside the bar */

/* keep the status text above the dots */
.cs-veil-line { position: relative; z-index: 2; }

/* optional: slightly round the ends of the strip so the beads “hug” it */
.cs-veil { border-radius: 8px; }

/* ——— HALO AROUND THE TOP NAV CONTAINER (auto-detect) ——— */
.wp-site-blocks > header
  :where(.wp-block-group,.wp-block-row,.wp-block-columns,.wp-block-cover)
  :has(> nav.wp-block-navigation) {
    position: relative !important;
    border-radius: 10px;
    isolation: isolate;
}

/* dotted mint ring */
.wp-site-blocks > header
  :where(.wp-block-group,.wp-block-row,.wp-block-columns,.wp-block-cover)
  :has(> nav.wp-block-navigation)::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    padding: 8px;                       /* halo thickness */
    border-radius: 12px;

background:
      radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.85) 2px, transparent 3px)
      0 0 / 18px 18px;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(169,255,232,.45));

/* keep dots only in the ring band */
    -webkit-mask:
      linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box,
      linear-gradient(#000 0 0);
    -webkit-mask-composite: xor;
            mask-composite: exclude;

pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 1;
}

/* keep nav content above the halo */
.wp-site-blocks > header
  :where(.wp-block-group,.wp-block-row,.wp-block-columns,.wp-block-cover)
  :has(> nav.wp-block-navigation) > * {
    position: relative; z-index: 2;
}

/* optional subtle drift */
@keyframes navHaloDrift { to { background-position: 18px 18px; } }
.wp-site-blocks > header
  :where(.wp-block-group,.wp-block-row,.wp-block-columns,.wp-block-cover)
  :has(> nav.wp-block-navigation)::before {
    animation: navHaloDrift 60s linear infinite;
}

.cs-veil-bg::before,
.cs-veil-bg::after { content:none !important; display:none !important; }

.cs-veil { position: relative !important; }

.cs-veil-halo {
  position: absolute !important;
  inset: 4px !important;
  border: 2px dotted #a9ffe8 !important;
  border-radius: 10px !important;
  opacity: .8 !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(169,255,232,.35),
              inset 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.15) !important;
  pointer-events: none !important;
  z-index: 2 !important;
}

.cs-veil-bg   { position: absolute; inset: -1px; z-index: 0 !important; }
.cs-veil-line { position: relative; z-index: 3 !important; }

.cs-submenu-wrap {
  position: relative;
  border: 1px dotted rgba(169,255,232,.35);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 6px 0;
  margin: 6px 0;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 8px rgba(169,255,232,.25),
    inset 0 0 10px rgba(169,255,232,.15);
}
[1760451499.464197] Assistant: Got it — we’ll just tighten the beads and make the row sit a touch closer to the bottom edge.  
Paste this **at the very end of your CSS** (it overrides your current `.cs-veil:after`):

```css
/* ── Veil bead row: tighter spacing + closer to bottom edge ── */
:root{
  --bead-size: 1.3px;                     /* dot radius-ish */
  --bead-gap: 11px;                       /* horizontal spacing */
  --bead-row: 9px;                        /* row height */
  --bead-color: rgba(169,255,232,.80);    /* mint */
  --bead-feather: 22px;                   /* fade at the ends */
  --bead-offset: 3px;                     /* how many px above the bottom */
}

.cs-veil { border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden; }

.cs-veil:after{
  content:"";
  position:absolute;
  inset:0;
  pointer-events:none;
  z-index:1;

/* 1) bottom beads  2) your scanlines */
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle, var(--bead-color) var(--bead-size), transparent calc(var(--bead-size) + .2px))
      left calc(100% - var(--bead-offset)) / var(--bead-gap) var(--bead-row) repeat-x,
    repeating-linear-gradient(0deg,
      rgba(255,255,255,.03) 0 1px,
      transparent 2px 4px);

/* feather ends so the row “hugs” rounded corners */
  -webkit-mask: linear-gradient(90deg,
      transparent 0 var(--bead-feather),
      #000 calc(var(--bead-feather) + 6px) calc(100% - (var(--bead-feather) + 6px)),
      transparent calc(100% - var(--bead-feather)));
          mask: linear-gradient(90deg,
      transparent 0 var(--bead-feather),
      #000 calc(var(--bead-feather) + 6px) calc(100% - (var(--bead-feather) + 6px)),
      transparent calc(100% - var(--bead-feather)));

/* soft glow under the beads */
  box-shadow:
    0 -1px 0 rgba(169,255,232,.25) inset,
    0 -8px 16px rgba(169,255,232,.14) inset;
}
```

### Tweak knobs (if you want)
- **Closer together:** lower `--bead-gap` (e.g., `9px`).
- **Smaller dots:** lower `--bead-size` (e.g., `1.0px`).
- **Closer to the edge:** lower `--bead-offset` (e.g., `2px`).
- **Softer ends:** raise `--bead-feather` (e.g., `28px`).

---

[1760451634.503061] Assistant: You’re running into **cascade order**: the `<style>` that lives **inside your header template** overrides anything in “Additional CSS”. So tweaks there won’t show until we either (a) replace the inline rule, or (b) dodge the cascade with a tiny extra element.

Fastest + bulletproof fix: **add one tiny element for the beads** and style just that. No pseudo-element fights.

### 1) Edit your header HTML
Inside the `.cs-veil` (right after the `<div class="cs-veil-line" …>…</div>`), add this one line:

```html
<div class="cs-dotrow" aria-hidden="true"></div>
```

So the block becomes:

```html
<div class="cs-veil" role="banner" aria-label="Attunement Console">
  <div class="cs-veil-bg"></div>
  <div class="cs-veil-symbols" aria-hidden="true">…</div>
  <div class="cs-veil-line" id="cs-veil-line">[Booting resonance layer…]</div>

<!-- mint bead row -->
  <div class="cs-dotrow" aria-hidden="true"></div>
</div>
```

### 2) In the **same inline `<style>`** (the one already in your header), paste this CSS (keep your other rules). It’s self-contained and wins the cascade:

```css
/* ── single-purpose bead row element ── */
.cs-dotrow{
  position: absolute;
  left: -8px;                 /* tiny overhang so ends feel rounded */
  right: -8px;
  bottom: -1px;               /* nudge closer to the edge */
  height: 10px;               /* row height */
  z-index: 1;                 /* above bg, below the status text */

background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.85) 1.2px, transparent 1.4px);
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  background-size: 12px 10px; /* ← spacing X | row height Y */
  background-position: left bottom;

filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(169,255,232,.35));
  pointer-events: none;
  mix-blend-mode: normal;
}

/* keep the text above the dots */
.cs-veil-line{ position: relative; z-index: 2; }
```

### Tweak knobs (edit right here)
- **Closer dots:** reduce `background-size: 10px 10px;`
- **Smaller dots:** change `1.2px` to `1.0px` in the radial-gradient.
- **Touching the edge:** change `bottom: -2px` or `0px` to taste.
- **Softer glow:** lower the drop-shadow alpha from `.35` to `.25`.

This avoids the pseudo-element conflicts and should immediately give you a crisp, adjustable bead row exactly where you want it.

.cs-dotrow{

  right: -8px;

  /* ——— rotating veil ——— */

}

@keyframes csShift {
    0% { background-position: 0%; }
    100% { background-position: 200%; }
  }
  .cs-veil-line {
    position: relative;
    padding: 0.6rem 2rem;
    font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", monospace;
    font-size: clamp(0.9rem, 1.5vw, 1.05rem);
    color: #b6ffe4;
    text-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(64, 255, 192, 0.25);
    letter-spacing: 0.2px;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;
    max-width: 100vw;
    animation: csGlow 4s ease-in-out infinite;
    z-index: 2;
    -webkit-mask-image: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0, #000 24px, #000 calc(100% - 24px), transparent 100%);
            mask-image: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0, #000 24px, #000 calc(100% - 24px), transparent 100%);
  }
  @keyframes csGlow {
    0%, 100% { opacity: 0.85; }
    50% { opacity: 1; }
  }
  /* scanlines + mint dot rows (top & bottom) on the SAME layer */
/* scanlines + tasteful mint bead row only at the bottom edge */
.cs-veil:after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 1;

/* layers: 1) bottom dots  2) existing scanlines */
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle, rgba(169,255,232,.70) 1.2px, transparent 1.3px)
      bottom left / 14px 11px repeat-x,
    repeating-linear-gradient(0deg,
      rgba(255,255,255,.03) 0 1px,
      transparent 2px 4px);

/* feather the ends so the row fades near the rounded corners */
  -webkit-mask: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 12px, #000 28px calc(100% - 28px), transparent calc(100% - 12px));
          mask: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 12px, #000 28px calc(100% - 28px), transparent calc(100% - 12px));

/* gentle glow under the bead row */
  box-shadow:
    0 -1px 0 rgba(169,255,232,.25) inset,
    0 -6px 14px rgba(169,255,232,.12) inset;
}

.cs-veil.sticky { position: sticky; top: 0; }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .cs-veil-bg, .cs-veil-line, .cs-veil-symbols { animation: none; }
  }
</style>
<script>
  (function(){
    const lines = [
      "[Decrypting field patterns…]",
      "[Resonance threshold passed]",
      "[Echo nodes: online]",
      "[Signal integrity: 99.97%]",
      "[Phase: 7/13 — synchronization in progress]",
      "[Awaiting user attunement…]",
      "[Glitch→Glyph: successful]",
      "[Paradox stabilized]"
    ];
    const el = document.getElementById("cs-veil-line");
    let i = 0;

function glitch() {
      el.style.transform = `translate(${Math.random()*4-2}px, ${Math.random()*2-1}px)`;
      el.style.filter = 'hue-rotate(180deg)';
      setTimeout(() => {
        el.style.transform = '';
        el.style.filter = '';
      }, 50);
    }

function swap(){
      // Random glitch 20% of the time
      if(Math.random() < 0.2) glitch();

i = (i+1) % lines.length;
      el.style.opacity = 0;
      setTimeout(()=>{ el.textContent = lines[i]; el.style.opacity = 1; }, 220);
    }
    setInterval(swap, 4200);
  })();
</script>

[1760451977.097279] Assistant: That “not all the way across” is the classic `100vw` + centered transform issue. `100vw` counts the scrollbar, so your strip ends a few px short on the right. Easy fix: make the veil use normal flow width.

Drop this in **Additional CSS** (or update the `<style>` in your header):

```css
/* Make the veil truly full-width of the page content (not 100vw) */
.cs-veil{
  width: 100% !important;
  left: 0 !important;
  transform: none !important;
}

/* Let the bead row span edge to edge of the veil */
.cs-dotrow{
  left: 0 !important;
  right: 0 !important;
}
```

If you ever want true **edge-to-edge** across the *window* (including margins) without the scrollbar gap, you can instead use the dynamic viewport unit:

```css
@supports (width: 100dvw){
  .cs-veil { width: 100dvw !important; left: 50% !important; transform: translateX(-50%) !important; }
}
```

(Stick with the first snippet for “make my OCD chill” — it’ll stretch that mint line all the way across.)

(function(){
  const lines = [

function glitch() {
    el.style.transform = `translate(${Math.random()*4-2}px, ${Math.random()*2-1}px)`;
    el.style.filter = 'hue-rotate(180deg)';
    setTimeout(() => { el.style.transform = ''; el.style.filter = ''; }, 50);
  }
  function swap(){
    if (Math.random() < 0.2) glitch();
    i = (i+1) % lines.length;
    el.style.opacity = 0;
    setTimeout(()=>{ el.textContent = lines[i]; el.style.opacity = 1; }, 220);
  }
  setInterval(swap, 4200);
})();
</script>

```html
  /* ——— rotating veil ——— */
  .cs-veil {
    position: relative;
    inset: 0;
    width: 100%; /* changed from 100vw for true full-width content alignment */

    min-height: 60px;

    position: absolute;
    inset: -1px;

    opacity: 0.9;

/* ——— scrolling symbols ——— */
  .cs-veil-symbols {
```
